How many people died after being diagnosed with posttraumatic stress disorder within 2 months throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id 
WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL 
AND admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
    AND strftime('%J', patients.dod) - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 2 * 30
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '<', '2 * 30'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )

How many patients died during the same hospital encounter after having been diagnosed in 2100 with varices of other sites?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN diagnoses_icd ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code = diagnoses_icd.icd_code 
JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id 
WHERE strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' 
AND d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'varices of other sites' 
AND admissions.discharge_location = 'died' 
AND admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id 
    AND admissions.discharge_location = 'died'
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['admissions.discharge_location', '=', 'died'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', 'patients.subject_id'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'varices of other sites'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:

What are the five most frequently prescribed medications for patients diagnosed with urethral stricture, unspecified previously within 2 months, this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urethral stricture, unspecified'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urethral stricture, unspecified'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

TRUTH:

Tell me about the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified') AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

)

    FROM diagnoses_icd

        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code

        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'postprocedural pneumothorax'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'

How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN diagnoses_icd ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code = diagnoses_icd.icd_code 
JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id 
WHERE admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL 
AND datetime(admissions.admittime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year') 
AND d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' 
AND patients.dod IS NOT NULL 
AND strftime('%J', patients.dod) > strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'facial weakness'], ["datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%j',patients.dod)", '>', "strftime('%j', diagnoses_icd.charttime)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime, T1.hadm_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE T2.hadm_id = T3.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.hadm_id', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'facial weakness'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ['t2.hadm_id', '=', 't3.hadm_id']]}

What are the four most commonly prescribed drugs for patients who were diagnosed with urinary incontinence, unspecified previously within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary incontinence, unspecified'
        )
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary incontinence, unspecified'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary incontinence, unspecified' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary incontinence, unspecified'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

How much does patient 10007058 change in o2 saturation pulseoxymetry second measured on the first ICU visit compared to the first value measured on the first ICU visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT 
    (SELECT chartevents.valuenum 
     FROM chartevents 
     WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN (
         SELECT icustays.stay_id 
         FROM icustays 
         WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN (
             SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
             FROM admissions 
             WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058 
             ORDER BY admissions.admittime ASC 
             LIMIT 1
         )
     ) 
     AND chartevents.itemid IN (
         SELECT d_items.itemid 
         FROM d_items 
         WHERE d_items.label = 'o2 saturation pulseoxymetry' 
         AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents'
     ) 
     ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC 
     LIMIT 1 OFFSET 1
    ) - 
    (SELECT chartevents.valuenum 
     FROM chartevents 
     WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN (
         SELECT icustays.stay_id 
         FROM icustays 
         WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN (
             SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
             FROM admissions 
             WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058 
             ORDER BY admissions.admittime ASC 
             LIMIT 1
         )
     ) 
     AND chartevents.itemid IN (
         SELECT d_items.itemid 
         FROM d_items 
         WHERE d_items.label = 'o2 saturation pulseoxymetry' 
         AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents'
     ) 
     ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC 
     LIMIT 1
    )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'chartevents.valuenum', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'chartevents', 'd_items', 'icustays'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10007058'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'o2 saturation pulseoxymetry'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT chartevents.valuenum FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058 ) AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'o2 saturation pulseoxymetry' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC LIMIT 1 OFFSET 1 ) - ( SELECT chartevents.valuenum FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058 ) AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'o2 saturation pulseoxymetry' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC LIMIT 1 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'chartevents.valuenum', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'chartevents', 'd_items', 'icustays'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10007058'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'o2 saturation pulseoxymetry'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents'], ['icustays.outtime', 'is', 'not null']]}

How much does the calcium, total lab test typically cost?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(T1.C1) FROM ( SELECT SUM(cost.cost) AS C1 FROM cost WHERE cost.hadm_id IN ( SELECT labevents.hadm_id FROM labevents WHERE labevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_labitems.itemid FROM d_labitems WHERE d_labitems.label = 'calcium, total' ) ) GROUP BY cost.hadm_id ) AS T1
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'calcium, total']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT DISTINCT cost.cost FROM cost WHERE cost.event_type = 'labevents' AND cost.event_id IN ( SELECT labevents.row_id FROM labevents WHERE labevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_labitems.itemid FROM d_labitems WHERE d_labitems.label = 'calcium, total' ) )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.row_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['cost.event_type', '=', 'labevents'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'calcium, total']]}

What was the average monthly number of patients who were diagnosed with accidental fall from bed this year?

----------

Count the number of patients for whom vial was prescribed within 2 months post their diagnosis of secondary and unspecified malignant neoplasm of intra-abdominal lymph nodes in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'secondary and unspecified malignant neoplasm of intra-abdominal lymph nodes'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime 
    FROM prescriptions 
    JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE prescriptions.dose_unit_rx = 'vial'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')

What were the top four most frequent microbiology tests that patients were given after being diagnosed with cerebral aneurysm, nonruptured during the same month in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM (
    SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral aneurysm, nonruptured'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y-%m', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100-06'
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM microbiologyevents
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE strftime('%Y-%m', microbiologyevents.charttime) = '2100-06'
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.test_name
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral aneurysm, nonruptured'], ["strftime('%y-%m',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100-06'], ["strftime('%y-%m',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '=', '2100-06'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM ( SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral aneurysm, nonruptured' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',microbiologyevents.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.test_name ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral aneurysm, nonruptured'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

Give me the top three most common diagnoses for patients of age 60 or above since 1 year ago.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title 
FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN (
    SELECT T1.icd_code 
    FROM (
        SELECT diagnoses_icd.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.hadm_id IN (
            SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
            FROM admissions 
            WHERE admissions.age >= 60 
            AND datetime(admissions.admittime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
        ) 
        GROUP BY diagnoses_icd.icd_code
    ) AS T1 
    WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
)

TRUTH:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 't1.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of alcoholic cirrhosis of liver within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT diagnoses_icd.subject_id) 
FROM diagnoses_icd 
JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code = diagnoses_icd.icd_code 
JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = diagnoses_icd.subject_id 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver' 
AND strftime('%Y-%m', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m', patients.dod) 
AND patients.dod IS NOT NULL
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['diagnoses_icd.subject_id'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver'], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%y-%m',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m', patients.dod)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')

Among patients who were diagnosed with acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous during this year, what are the top five most commonly prescribed medications that followed in the same hospital encounter for patients in their 50s?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What is the 3-month survival probability for patients living with a asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status') AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

What is the average yearly number of patients who had encounter for palliative care?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(yearly_patient_count) FROM (
  SELECT strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) AS year, COUNT(DISTINCT diagnoses_icd.subject_id) AS yearly_patient_count
  FROM diagnoses_icd
  JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON diagnoses_icd.icd_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
  WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'encounter for palliative care'
  GROUP BY year
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.subject_id'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'encounter for palliative care']]}
----------

Get me the top five most frequent specimens that patients were tested within 2 months after receiving bypass coronary artery, one artery from aorta with autologous venous tissue, open approach since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc 
FROM (
    SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
        FROM procedures_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_procedures 
            WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'bypass coronary artery, one artery from aorta with autologous venous tissue, open approach'
        ) 
        AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime 
        FROM microbiologyevents 
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
    AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month') 
    GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'bypass coronary artery, one artery from aorta with autologous venous tissue, open approach'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'bypass coronary artery, one artery from aorta with autologous venous tissue, open approach' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',microbiologyevents.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'bypass coronary artery, one artery from aorta with autologous venous tissue, open approach'], ["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What are the three most frequently ordered medications that f patients aged 60 or above were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age >= 60
        AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age >= 60 AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------

When was the first time patient 10018328 had been prescribed enalapril maleate and amlodipine at the same time since 02/2100?
----------

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of septic shock within 2 months during the last year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT diagnoses_icd.subject_id) 
FROM diagnoses_icd 
JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code = diagnoses_icd.icd_code 
JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = diagnoses_icd.subject_id 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'septic shock' 
AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time, '-1 year') 
AND strftime('%J', patients.dod) - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 2 * 365 / 12 
AND strftime('%J', current_time) - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 2 * 365 / 12
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['diagnoses_icd.subject_id'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'septic shock'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2 * 365 / 12'], ["strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '<', '2 * 365 / 12'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'septic shock' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'septic shock'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

Count the number of patients who had insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage within 2 months after having been diagnosed with home accidents since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'home accidents'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage'
    )
) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'home accidents'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'home accidents' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'home accidents'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

    FROM prescriptions

For patients diagnosed with unspecified disorder of kidney and ureter, what were the top three most common microbiology tests that followed in the same hospital encounter since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM ( SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified disorder of kidney and ureter' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',microbiologyevents.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.test_name ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified disorder of kidney'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

What are the three most commonly prescribed drugs for patients who were diagnosed with personal history of tobacco use previously in the same month, since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of tobacco use'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'personal history of tobacco use'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ["datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t1.charttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of tobacco use' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'personal history of tobacco use'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to f patients in their 50s during the same hospital encounter after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND admissions.subject_id IN (
            SELECT patients.subject_id
            FROM patients
            WHERE patients.gender = 'f'
        )
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to m patients in their 30s during the same month after they were diagnosed with hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia during this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
        AND admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hyposmolality'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

What are the four most frequently ordered medications that m patients aged 20s were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with mitral valve disorders since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

What are the three most frequent drugs prescribed to f patients with an age of 20s within the same month after they were diagnosed with unspecified acquired hypothyroidism?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'
        ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.subject_id IN (
            SELECT patients.subject_id
            FROM patients
            WHERE patients.gender = 'f'
        )
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

How many patients underwent other electric countershock of heart within 2 months after the diagnosis with personal history of tobacco use?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT procedures_icd.subject_id) 
FROM procedures_icd 
WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code IN (
    SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
    FROM d_icd_procedures 
    WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'other electric countershock of heart'
) 
AND procedures_icd.charttime <= datetime(
    (SELECT MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) 
     FROM diagnoses_icd 
     WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code IN (
         SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
         FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
         WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of tobacco use'
     ) 
     AND diagnoses_icd.subject_id = procedures_icd.subject_id
    ), '+2 month'
) 
AND procedures_icd.charttime >= (
    SELECT MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code IN (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of tobacco use'
    ) 
    AND diagnoses_icd.subject_id = procedures_icd.subject_id
)

----------
TRUTH:

----------
PREDS:

FROM admissions 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT prescriptions.hadm_id 
    FROM prescriptions 
    WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'ferric gluconate'

        SELECT MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'ankylosing spondylitis'
        ) 
        AND diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id
    ) AND datetime(
        (SELECT MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'ankylosing spondylitis'
        ) 
        AND diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id), 
        '+2 month'
    )
) 
AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100'

What is the 3-month survival probability for patients living with a asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status diagnosis?
